## What wombat is

A quiet, deterministic personal assistant — "The Steward" — where the **LLM is a mouth, not a brain**. Built as a pure **adopter of the local cog-worx** cognitive framework (Stages + pathways on its Engine; never forks the Engine). The product thesis rides on a deterministic **interruption gate**: a two-axis **Urgency × Cognitive Load** pressure engine with **no LLM in the decision**.

## The locked spine (DEC-1..DEC-19 — do not change without escalation)

- **Gate (DEC-1/13):** two-axis Urgency × Cognitive Load, deterministic code between the queue and the loop; per-event ratings, personalized + adaptive; Cognitive Load cumulative across a durable pending set.
- **cog-worx mapping (DEC-12):** wombat = Stages + pathways on cog-worx's Engine; no loop code, no forked Engine.
- **Loop (DEC-8/14):** one queue-driven drain pathway; pluggable input sources register with a source registry; idles on empty.
- **Memory (DEC-11, supersedes DEC-2; DEC-15):** cog-worx user_model scope + a thin PA empirical-user-model layer; behavior + outcomes only, never motive. The dev agent-memory pipeline is a *separate, out-of-scope* layer.
- **Mouth (DEC-4/18):** DeepSeek (cloud, OpenAI-compat) via the cog-worx S4 seam, behind the budget guard; renders pre-decided output only.
- **Taint invariant (DEC-26, v0.74):** `taint_drops_external` is INVARIANT TRUE across all wombat code — no stage may ever construct/bind a `StageToolPolicy(taint_drops_external=False)` (the TK-148 verifier's 8th probe proved a False re-bind after latch re-exposes external tools). Structural guard lands with TK-151; until then every policy-touching briefing (TK-149/TK-153/brief-cluster) carries it as a named binding constraint. Relaxing it ever = Jim decision.
- **Brief & dreaming (DEC-16/17/23):** morning brief = scheduled pathway = one forced flush through the gate (selects its own items via the gate's threshold-free `select_items` seam, never the live pending set); nightly "dreaming" = off-path batch pathway that re-tunes ratings. **DEC-23:** off-path (S1) consolidation-time LLM inference IS admitted in the dream pathway (the cog-worx reconciler/extractor use a model-backed oracle) — the GATE stays model-free (NG-4 governs the gate, not off-path consolidation; bounded by a BudgetGuard).
- **Side effects (DEC-19):** journal trail + review-before-send via `AwaitHuman` and the cog-worx approval gate.
- **External tier (DEC-20 + DEC-22):** the cog-worx `external` capability tier is admitted ONLY for the approved AwaitHuman dispatch stages (Gmail draft, browser); engine default stays `{read,write}`. The planning **authorization is GRANTED** (DEC-22, settled by architect under Jim's delegation). The only remaining act is the **build-time enablement** of the cog-worx external-tier flag in deploy config — a TK-151 bring-up step, not a planning blocker; TK-151 fails closed until that flag is set (wombat never self-grants at runtime).
- **Wombat-day (DEC-21):** the canonical day is the **civil-local date** in a configured IANA timezone, computed at access time (survives laptop sleep across midnight); one shared `DailyLedger` primitive (TK-152) owns the boundary for spend/ceiling/brief ledgers.
- **Scope (DEC-3/6/7/9/10):** v1 = read-only Google Calendar + Gmail-only → one combined morning brief; voice + browser in v1; runs on a laptop.
- **Voice providers (DEC-28, v1.64):** STT/TTS are pluggable slots behind the EXISTING Transcriber/TTSAdapter seams; defaults local+free (faster-whisper/pyttsx3, zero-config = fully offline). Cloud voice (ElevenLabs/Deepgram/Fish Audio) is **structural per-provider user opt-in** (config selection AND user-supplied key, or no construction); degrade strictly cloud→local, never local→cloud.
- **Front-end direction (DEC-31, Jim-decided):** the Electron + React app (settings surface in v1, DEC-29 zero-analytics / DEC-30 config-surface-only) is intended to grow into wombat's front-end/companion surface (future 3D assistant avatar). The loopback token-guarded Python settings API is its entire backend (DEC-32 config tiers: keyring secrets / settings.json non-secrets / .env operator-owned; restart-to-apply).
- **Persona (DEC-33..36, v1.81, Jim-approved):** the personality matrix is **5 axes as named enum levels** (brevity/warmth/directness/humor/proactivity; DEFAULT = today's steward, byte-identical prompts) rendered by a **pure-function clause-algebra builder** with an immutable guard suffix; **hot-apply via LivePersona** (DEC-34, Jim authority — supersedes-in-part DEC-32's restart-to-apply for IN-PROCESS persona actuation only); voice tuning is a **deterministic CLOSED grammar intercepted pre-queue at ASRSource** (DEC-35 — never enqueued/gate-rated/mouth-rendered); learning is **explicit feedback tokens only**, aggregated nightly by dream_persona under RatingTuner-pattern custody (DEC-36; implicit tone-adaptation = DEF-8). Constitution-excluded axes are NAMED as excluded (interruption-eagerness, motive-empathy, persuasion/sycophancy, action-initiative). Persona shapes only HOW gate-cleared output reads — never whether/when anything surfaces.

- **Persona fidelity restoration (DEC-37, v1.84):** the RECOVERED full Jim-approved proposal supersedes the v1.81 summary-based record where they diverge — proactivity actuates as a **bounded deterministic `urgency_threshold` offset inside a recorded `personality_band` in `wombat_params.yaml`** (TK-215; gate-constant custody, CON-3/NG-3 intact); degrade-path brevity/warmth variants + recorded directness/humor model-path-only asymmetry (TK-216); reflection = the FOURTH persona mouth (its CON-6/NG-1 bars ARE the immutable guard_suffix); humor = none|dry only, never in drafts/reflection; deterministic one-line command ack; settings-app persona edits hot-apply via mtime poll; ≥2-signal nightly threshold + 7-day explicit-set pin.
- **Persona expression redesign (DEC-38, v1.97, Jim directive — "be bold"):** the prompt algorithm becomes a swappable STRATEGY seam `render(mouth, matrix, cues) -> Expression`; humor is UNBARRED structurally — per-mouth persona behavior is POLICY DATA in a versioned operator-editable `persona_policy.yaml` (params custody; default policy = today's placement byte-exactly; supersedes-in-part DEC-37(c)); the cues bag is typed+extensible (growth axes mood/scene/temporal/tone/INTONATION, v1 empty); Expression is a TYPE shaped to grow TTS delivery hints (v1 instruction text only); the TK-210 eval permitted-set derives from the SAME loaded policy (no silent tweaks). Five invariants survive ANY strategy, SEAM-enforced: purity/determinism, DEFAULT byte-identity, guard_suffix unremovable by any strategy or cue, measurable-or-flagged (never placebo). Carried by TK-219 → TK-220 → TK-221 (+ TK-210 re-land).
- **App rescope (DEC-39, v1.98, Jim directive):** the Electron app grows beyond settings — basic CHAT riding the full queue/gate/mouth discipline (runtime-owned loopback+token ChatSource surface; NO side-channel to the model; supersedes-in-part DEC-31's entire-backend + DEC-30's config-surface-ONLY clauses), mic/audio controls on the existing drop-dir/ASRSource path (output volume EXCLUDED v1 — the no-placebo bar extends to controls), and BINDING design requirements (bright color-theory tokenized palette, complete Tailwind theme — never MUI, no gradients, icons, minimal animation, scale, not-ai-slop). DEC-29 zero-analytics reaffirmed on every new surface. Carried by TK-222..TK-225 + TK-198/TK-200 amendments.

## Persona design — the full recovered intent (FEAT-14, DEC-33..37)

The v1.81 record was reconstructed from an orchestrator summary after a power cut; the proposing architect's FULL Jim-approved proposal was later recovered and DEC-37 (v1.84) restored the dropped/inverted substance. The complete design, in one place, so it can never be lost again:

- **Matrix (DEC-33 + DEC-37):** five closed named-level axes — brevity terse|balanced|expansive, warmth reserved|neutral|warm, directness gentle|plain|blunt, humor **none|dry** (two levels; the v1.81 "playful" was recorder-added, struck), proactivity minimal|balanced|forward. DEFAULT = (terse, reserved, plain, none, **balanced**) = today's steward prompts byte-for-byte AND today's gate exactly (balanced = zero offset). Excluded axes, NAMED so absence is a decision: interruption-eagerness (CON-2/3), empathy-as-motive-inference (CON-6), persuasion/sycophancy (CON-1), action-initiative (CON-5), coaching/clinical register (NG-2), persistence/reminder-frequency (NG-3).
- **Prompt actuation (TK-206/207):** one pure clause-algebra builder over FOUR mouths (compose, brief, draft, reflection) — base_role + length/register/hedging/humor clauses + an IMMUTABLE guard_suffix present at every matrix value (reflection's CON-6/NG-1 motive/clinical bars ARE its suffix). Humor renders ONLY for compose/brief — never Gmail drafts (the user's outbound voice) or reflection. Exhaustive 162-combination property tests; DEFAULT byte-identity pinned against the live constants.
- **Gate actuation (DEC-37a, TK-215):** proactivity = a bounded deterministic offset on `urgency_threshold` — a `personality_band` block in `wombat_params.yaml` (minimal +0.10 / balanced 0.00 / forward −0.10, PROVISIONAL under DEF-1; hard floor 0.60 / cap 0.95; human-edited, versioned, same custody as every gate constant — no tuner or runtime path writes it; composes with RatingTuner, the band bounds the COMBINED effect). CON-3 intact — no axis creates a surfacing, every surfaced item still clears the deterministic gate; NG-3 intact — no cap/ceiling ever raised (minimal may only lower willingness); zero LLM. Read via LivePersona at scoring time (next-scored-item semantics).
- **Degrade actuation (DEC-37e, TK-216):** brevity wrapper variants in TemplateComposer + the brief's fixed lines (terse = today's exact bytes); warm adds ONE fixed greeting line to the brief degrade; directness/humor have NO degrade variant — recorded honest asymmetry, model-path-only, pinned by test.
- **Hot-apply & custody (DEC-34 + DEC-37g, TK-209):** LivePersona is the ONE runtime authority; mouths + gate read at render/scoring time; set() applies next turn and persists to `wombat.settings.json` (the single persisted home; never .env, never secrets). Voice commands refresh synchronously; settings-APP writes hot-apply too via an mtime poll on the existing timer beat (settings API keeps zero-runtime-imports, TK-197 untouched; last-write-wins acceptable on the single-user file). Providers/keys/name stay restart-to-apply (DEC-32/34). TK-200's persona selects show hot-apply, not the restart notice.
- **Voice commands (DEC-35 + DEC-37f, TK-211/212):** closed versioned utterance grammar, deterministic whole-utterance normalized matching (no LLM/embeddings/fuzzy — CON-1); intercepted at ASRSource AFTER transcription, BEFORE enqueue — never queued/gate-rated/mouth-rendered; unmatched speech flows byte-unchanged; trail line BEFORE apply (CON-4), no AwaitHuman; ends with ONE deterministic one-line ack ("Warmth is now warm.") via the existing speak/degrade path, zero model calls.
- **Learning (DEC-36 + DEC-37h, TK-213/214 + DEF-8):** explicit closed-lexicon feedback tokens only (the TK-51 grammar discipline; motive-free `persona_feedback` events) → nightly `dream_persona` AFTER `dream_tune` under RatingTuner-pattern custody: ≥2 consistent in-window signals, at most ONE clamped step per axis per night, journaled human-readably; an explicitly user-set axis is PINNED against nudges for 7 days (explicit always outranks learning). Implicit tone-adaptation stays deferred (DEF-8).
- **Eval bar (TK-210, EP-33's done-bar):** env-gated live-eval harness measures OUTPUT effects per level (brevity monotonicity, warmth greeting presence/absence, directness hedge-lexicon, humor only-where-permitted + asserted ABSENT from draft/reflection, forbidden-term classifiers at all levels); proactivity is proven deterministically at the gate (TK-215), exempt from sampling; any unmeasurable axis gets FLAGGED in governance, never shipped as placebo.
- **Redesign layer (DEC-38, v1.97) + measurability record (ISS-7, v1.96):** the first armed TK-210 run proved humor(dry) and brevity(balanced) clause texts too WEAK to move the live mouth — adjudicated as clause-strength, not concept; the fix rides the DEC-38 policy-data surface (TK-221 calibration) and TK-210's amended symmetric-fail-loud ACs. **TK-221 landed 2026-07-10 (v2.02): both axes now separate live under the unweakened N=9 rule — the measurability finding is DISCHARGED; the non-separation contingency never triggered.** The clause algebra survives as the v1 DEFAULT strategy behind the new seam; the per-mouth humor walls survive as the DEFAULT POLICY (data), no longer code.
- **Sequencing:** TK-196 → EP-33 (now incl. TK-219/220/221 → TK-210 done-bar) → EP-34 → EP-32 remainder (now incl. chat/audio/design TK-222..225) → EP-35 (P3); browser EP-25/26 stay P3 behind all of it.

## v1 scope boundary

IN: read-only calendar, Gmail-only (incl. drafts via review-before-send), one combined morning brief, voice input source, browser/computer use, laptop deployment, pluggable voice providers (local default, opt-in cloud — DEC-28), the local Electron app (settings + basic chat riding queue/gate/mouth + mic/audio controls + the binding tokenized design system — DEC-29/30/31/32 as rescoped by DEC-39; output-volume honestly excluded, no analytics ever), the tunable personality matrix (FEAT-14, Jim-approved v1.81 + the v1.84 fidelity restoration — DEC-33..37, incl. the gate personality_band TK-215 and degrade deltas TK-216; sequenced EP-33 → EP-34 → EP-32 remainder → EP-35).
OUT / deferred: always-on hardware, non-Gmail email, sending without review, the dev agent-memory RAG pipeline, accounts/multi-tenancy/packaging/installer/auto-update (DEC-30), the 3D avatar/companion rendering (flagged future, DEC-31), cloud-provider live smokes until Jim's keys (DEF-7), automatic tone-adaptation from implicit behavior (DEF-8 — persona learns only from explicit feedback tokens, DEC-36).

## Posture

Local-data scoped to **storage**; **exactly one egress in the DEFAULT configuration — the DeepSeek phrasing call** (ASMP-1 accepted 2026-06-20). **User-opted-in voice providers are additional permitted egress (DEC-28, v1.64)** — opt-in is structural (config selection + user-supplied key), degrade is strictly cloud→local, and TK-195 pins the default-zero-voice-egress invariant. S4 local-model swap stays the deferred escape hatch.
